## 📝 Duplicate Page Titles

Pages should have a descriptive, unique `<title>` so users can tell them apart in browser tabs, history, and bookmarks ([WCAG 2.4.2](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/page-titled.html)).

- **"Report a hedgehog sighting"** — 5 pages:
  - https://mgifford.github.io/record-a-goose-sighting/hedgehog/

## 🧭 Reflow Risk (WCAG 1.4.10)

Page-level indicator: resizes to 320 CSS pixels wide and checks for horizontal overflow. This is a risk indicator, not a conformance verdict — see [Behavioral Accessibility Automation](https://github.com/mgifford/ACCESSIBILITY.md/blob/main/examples/BEHAVIORAL_ACCESSIBILITY_AUTOMATION.md) for what it can and cannot detect.

## 🔍 Cross-Page Patterns: Common HTML Issues

These HTML patterns cause the same accessibility errors across multiple pages. **Fix the pattern once in your codebase to fix it everywhere!**

### 🎯 Top Patterns to Fix (Highest Impact)

#### Pattern 1: Affects 3 page(s) - 4 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: axe
**Rule**: [button-name](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.14/button-name?application=playwright)
**Impact**: critical
**Issue**: Buttons must have discernible text

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<button type="button" id="target" class="fixture-control"></button>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
iframe
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('iframe')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:

- https://mgifford.github.io/record-a-goose-sighting/lab/frames/nested-frame/
- https://mgifford.github.io/record-a-goose-sighting/lab/frames/two-frames/

**Scanner**: axe
**Rule**: [landmark-unique](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.14/landmark-unique?application=playwright)
**Impact**: moderate

main

#### Pattern 5: Affects 2 page(s) - 2 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: axe
**Rule**: [aria-roles](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.14/aria-roles?application=playwright)
**Impact**: critical
**Issue**: ARIA roles used must conform to valid values

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<div id="test-target" role="goose" tabindex="0">Review paca sighting</div>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
#test-host,#test-target
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser

4. Run: `$x('#test-host,#test-target')`
5. The element will be highlighted

- https://mgifford.github.io/record-a-goose-sighting/paca/
- https://mgifford.github.io/record-a-goose-sighting/paca/?role=invalid&shadow=open

## 🧪 ACT rule coverage

Grouped by W3C ACT rule, so engines can be compared on the same test. An engine that reports nothing is silent, not passing; single-engine findings are candidates for either a false positive or a missed detection and need review. Findings include observations whose target could not be resolved; the target count includes only resolved targets.

### [97a4e1 — Button has non-empty accessible name](https://www.w3.org/WAI/standards-guidelines/act/rules/97a4e1/)

- W3C ACT rule: [97a4e1](https://www.w3.org/WAI/standards-guidelines/act/rules/97a4e1/)
- WCAG: 4.1.2
- Engines mapped: axe, alfa, equalAccess, accesslint
- Engines reporting: axe, alfa, equalAccess, accesslint
- Findings: 48 across 13 resolved target(s)
- Coverage: multiple-engines

| Engine | Mapping | Findings |
|---|---|---:|
| axe | `aria-command-name` (verified), `button-name` (verified) | 14 |
| alfa | `sia-r12` (verified) | 14 |
